stock ActiveOil(playerid)
{
	if(Team[playerid]!=MERCENARY)
	{
		if(UnderAttack[OIL] == 0)
		{
			if(!IsPlayerInAnyVehicle(playerid))
		 	{
		 	    for(new num = 0; num < 4; num ++)
				{ TextDrawShowForPlayer(playerid, ZoneTextdraw_[num][playerid]); }

			 	UnderAttack[OIL] = 1;
			 	timer[playerid][OIL] = SetTimerEx("Oil", 25000, false,"i",playerid);
			 	Captured[playerid][OIL] = 0;
			 	SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,""COL_GREEN"Server: "COL_WHITE"Stay in the checkpoint for 25 seconds to capture zone.");
			 	PlayAudioStreamForPlayer(playerid, "http://files.mboxdrive.com/100000435279790/capturing.MP3");
             	if(Team[playerid] == ASIA)
			    {
				  GangZoneFlashForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_ASIA_COLOR);
				}
				else if(Team[playerid] == AFRICA)
				{
			      GangZoneFlashForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_AFRICA_COLOR);
			    }
			    else if(Team[playerid] == AMERICA)
			    {
			      GangZoneFlashForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_AMERICA_COLOR);
				}
				else if(Team[playerid] == EUROPE)
				{
			      GangZoneFlashForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_EUROPE_COLOR);
				}
				else if(Team[playerid] == AUSTRALIA)
				{
			      GangZoneFlashForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_AUSTRALIA_COLOR);
				}
				//------Message-----
			    if(tCP[OIL] == ASIA)
			    {
			      SendClientMessage(playerid, -1, ""COL_WHITE"The Zone is controlled by team "COL_ORANGE"Asia");
			      SendTeamMessage(ASIA, -1,"*Oil Factory is under attack!");
			    }
			    else if(tCP[OIL] == AFRICA)
			    {
			      SendClientMessage(playerid, -1, ""COL_WHITE"The Zone is controlled by team "COL_MAGENTA"Africa");
			      SendTeamMessage(AFRICA, -1,"*Oil Factory is under attack!");
			    }
			    else if(tCP[OIL] == AMERICA)
			    {
			      SendClientMessage(playerid, -1, ""COL_WHITE"The Zone is controlled by team "COL_VIOLET"America");
			      SendTeamMessage(AMERICA, -1,"*Oil Factory is under attack!");
				}
				else if(tCP[OIL] == EUROPE)
				{
				  SendClientMessage(playerid, -1, ""COL_WHITE"The Zone is controlled by team "COL_BLUE"Europe");
				  SendTeamMessage(EUROPE, -1,"*Oil Factory is under attack!");
				}
				else if(tCP[OIL] == AUSTRALIA)
				{
			      SendClientMessage(playerid, -1, ""COL_WHITE"The Zone is controlled by team "COL_GREEN"Australia");
			      SendTeamMessage(AUSTRALIA, -1,"*Oil Factory is under attack!");
				}
				else if(tCP[OIL] == TEAM_NONE)
				{
			      SendClientMessage(playerid, -1, ""COL_WHITE"This zone is not controlled by any team.");
				}
				//---------loop-------//
				for(new i = 0; i < MAX_PLAYERS; i ++)
				{
				   IsPlayerCapturing[i][OIL] = 1;
				}
			}
			else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 1);
		}
		else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 2);
	}
	else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 3);
	return 1;
}
stock OilCaptured(playerid)
{
    for(new num = 0; num < 4; num ++)
	{ TextDrawHideForPlayer(playerid, ZoneTextdraw_[num][playerid]); }

	Captured[playerid][OIL] = 1;
	UnderAttack[OIL] = 0;
	KillTimer(timer[playerid][OIL]);

    CountVar[playerid][OIL] = 25;
    GivePlayerScore(playerid, 5);
    GivePlayerMoney(playerid, 5000);
	SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,""COL_CYAN"Info: "COL_WHITE"Congratulations, you have capturesd \"Oil Factory\" "COL_RED"( +5 scores and +$5000 "COL_RED" ");
	PlayAudioStreamForPlayer(playerid, "http://files.mboxdrive.com/100000435279790/Captured%20success.MP3");
	//==========================================================================
	for(new i = 0; i < MAX_PLAYERS; i++)
	{
	   IsPlayerCapturing[i][OIL] = 0;
	   if(Team[i] == Team[playerid] && i != playerid && Team[i]==ADMIN)
	   {
		   SendClientMessage(i, 0xFFFFFFFF,""COL_GREEN">> "COL_WHITE" team has captured "COL_RED"Oil Factory" ""COL_RED"( +2 score"COL_RED" %s "COL_WHITE"has captured");
		   GivePlayerScore(i, 2);
	   }
	}
	//==========================================================================
	tCP[OIL] = Team[playerid];
	GangZoneStopFlashForAll(Zone[OIL]);
	//==========================================================================
	if(Team[playerid] == ASIA)
    {
	   GangZoneShowForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_ASIA_COLOR);
	}
	else if(Team[playerid] == AFRICA)
	{
       GangZoneShowForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_AFRICA_COLOR);
	}
	else if(Team[playerid] == AMERICA)
	{
	   GangZoneShowForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_AMERICA_COLOR);
	}
	else if(Team[playerid] == EUROPE)
	{
	   GangZoneShowForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_EUROPE_COLOR);
	}
	else if(Team[playerid] == AUSTRALIA)
    {
	   GangZoneShowForAll(Zone[OIL], ZONE_AUSTRALIA_COLOR);
    }
    //==========================================================================
    new str[128];
    format(str, sizeof(str),""COL_RED" %s "COL_WHITE"has captured \"Oil Factory\" for team %s", PlayerName(playerid), GetTeamName(playerid));
    SendClientMessageToAll(-1, str);
	return 1;
}
stock LeavingOil(playerid)
{
    for(new num = 0; num < 4; num ++)
	{ TextDrawHideForPlayer(playerid, ZoneTextdraw_[num][playerid]); }
    
	Captured[playerid][OIL] = 1;
	UnderAttack[OIL] = 0;
	KillTimer(timer[playerid][OIL]);

    CountVar[playerid][OIL] = 25;
    GangZoneStopFlashForAll(Zone[OIL]);
    for(new i = 0; i < MAX_PLAYERS; i++)
	{
	   IsPlayerCapturing[i][OIL] = 0;
	}
	SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,"*"COL_WHITE"You have been failed to capture this zone.");
	PlayAudioStreamForPlayer(playerid, "http://files.mboxdrive.com/100000435279790/failed%20capture.MP3");
	return 1;
}
forward Oil(playerid);
public Oil(playerid)
{
	OilCaptured(playerid);
	return 1;
}

//==============================================================================
//============================[ OnPlayerEnter and OnPlayerLeave Dynamic CP]=====
//==============================================================================
public OnPlayerEnterDynamicCP(playerid, checkpointid)
{
	if(checkpointid == CP[SNAKE])
	{
		if(IsLogged[playerid] == 1) {
			if(UnderAttack[SNAKE] == 0) {
				if(Team[playerid] != MERCENARY) {
					if(tCP[SNAKE] != Team[playerid]) {
						CountVar[playerid][SNAKE] = 25;
						ActiveSnakeFarm(playerid);
					} else return SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,"*"COL_WHITE"This zone is already captured by your team.");
				} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 3);
			} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 2);
		} else return 0;
	}
	else if(checkpointid == CP[BAY])
	{
        if(IsLogged[playerid] == 1) {
			if(UnderAttack[BAY] == 0) {
				if(Team[playerid] != MERCENARY) {
					if(tCP[BAY] != Team[playerid]) {
						CountVar[playerid][BAY] = 25;
						ActiveBay(playerid);
					} else return SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,"*"COL_WHITE"This zone is already captured by your team.");
				} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 3);
			} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 2);
		} else return 0;
	}
	else if(checkpointid == CP[BIG])
	{
        if(IsLogged[playerid] == 1) {
			if(UnderAttack[BIG] == 0) {

						ActiveQuarry(playerid);
					} else return SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,"*"COL_WHITE"This zone is already captured by your team.");
				} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 3);
			} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 2);
		} else return 0;
	}
	else if(checkpointid == CP[GUEST])
	{
		if(IsLogged[playerid] == 1) {
			if(UnderAttack[GUEST] == 0) {
				if(Team[playerid] != MERCENARY) {
					if(tCP[GUEST] != Team[playerid]) {
						CountVar[playerid][GUEST] = 25;
						ActiveGuest(playerid);
					} else return SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,"*"COL_WHITE"This zone is already captured by your team.");
				} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 3);
			} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 2);
		} else return 0;
	}
	else if(checkpointid == CP[EAR])
	{
		if(IsLogged[playerid] == 1) {
			if(UnderAttack[EAR] == 0) {
				if(Team[playerid] != MERCENARY) {
					if(tCP[EAR] != Team[playerid]) {
						CountVar[playerid][EAR] = 25;
						ActiveEar(playerid);
					} else return SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,"*"COL_WHITE"This zone is already captured by your team.");
				} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 3);
			} else return CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, 2);
		} else return 0;
	}
	return 1;
}

public OnPlayerLeaveDynamicCP(playerid, checkpointid)
{
    if(checkpointid == CP[SNAKE] && Captured[playerid][SNAKE] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][SNAKE] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[SNAKE]))
	{
		LeavingSnakeFarm(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[BAY] && Captured[playerid][BAY] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][BAY] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[BAY]))
	{
		LeavingBay(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[BIG] && Captured[playerid][BIG] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][BIG] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[BIG]))
	{
		LeavingArea51(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[ARMY] && Captured[playerid][ARMY] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][ARMY] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[ARMY]))
	{
		LeavingArmy(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[PETROL] && Captured[playerid][PETROL] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][PETROL] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[PETROL]))
	{
		LeavingPetrol(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[OIL] && Captured[playerid][OIL] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][OIL] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[OIL]))
	{
		LeavingOil(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[DESERT] && Captured[playerid][DESERT] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][DESERT] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[DESERT]))
	{
		LeavingDesert(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[QUARRY] && Captured[playerid][QUARRY] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][QUARRY] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[QUARRY]))
	{
		LeavingQuarry(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[GUEST] && Captured[playerid][GUEST] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][GUEST] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[GUEST]))
	{
		LeavingGuest(playerid);
	}
	if(checkpointid == CP[EAR] && Captured[playerid][EAR] == 0 && IsPlayerCapturing[playerid][EAR] == 1 && !IsPlayerInDynamicCP(playerid, CP[EAR]))
	{
		LeavingEar(playerid);
	}
	return 1;
}

stock CaptureZoneMessage(playerid, messageid)
{
	switch(messageid)
	{
	   case 1: SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,""COL_RED"Error: "COL_WHITE"You cannot capture while in vehicle.");
	   case 2: SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,""COL_RED"Error: "COL_WHITE"This zone is already being taken over.");
	   case 3: SendClientMessage(playerid, -1,""COL_RED"Error: "COL_WHITE"Team Mercenary cannot capture zones.");
	}
	return 1;
}

stock SendTeamMessage(teamid, color, string[])
{
	for(new x=0; x < MAX_PLAYERS; x++)
  	{
		if(IsPlayerConnected(x))
		{
	  		if(Team[x] == teamid)
	  		{
				SendClientMessage(x, color, string);
	  		}
		}
  	}
  	return 1;
}

stock GivePlayerScore(playerid, score)
{
	SetPlayerScore(playerid, GetPlayerScore(playerid)+score);
 	return 1;
}

		    if(tCP[SNAKE]==AFRICA)

		    if(tCP[SNAKE]==AMERICA)

stock GetTeamName(playerid)
{
	new str[66];
	if(Team[playerid] == ASIA) str =  ("Asia");
	else if(Team[playerid] == AFRICA) str =  ("Africa");
	else if(Team[playerid] == AMERICA) str =  ("America");
	else if(Team[playerid] == EUROPE) str =  ("Europe");
	else if(Team[playerid] == AUSTRALIA) str =  ("Australia");
	return str;
}
